# Current Producer Schema Resource Integration Audit

Readonly; no production/native edits. Bounded traversal starts ONLY from the eight explicit domain/aggregate mutation roots named by current mutation-authority projection, derives their seven actual plugin search roots by the existing producer policy, and applies that producer's directory exclusions. This is a complete snapshot of those declared search roots and their reachable selected leaf obligations, **not a complete mounted consumer census for every unregistered/default MutationLeaf elsewhere**. Compiler-source registry integration must resolve additional actual consumers from its owned source graph; this report must not be used to claim whole-repository closure.

Current full producer implementation/package glue/Cargo/authority and relevant original leaf/source/aggregate/native fixture cohorts captured, with full receipts below. No captured fixture was reduced to a keyword snippet. The temporary resource-census JSON preserves all scanned index rows, exact IDs, errors/conflicts, complete leaf payload records, and reference origins/candidate paths; selected raw documents captured individually.

## Bounded Current Resource Facts

- 4463 JSON files, 11701040 raw bytes examined under seven declared plugin search roots. No malformed UTF-8/JSON or duplicate decoded members observed in this bounded set (independent Python decoder with duplicate member hook).
- Whole declared search roots: 2557 Draft07 declarations exactly `http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#`; 55 2020 declarations exactly `https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema`; remaining files include descriptors/data without dialect declaration. The old Rust index does not discriminate these schemas versus any `$id`-bearing data.
- Whole search-root reference-string shapes: 2606 absolute, 5283 local, 1 relative (all-object scan, not certified schema-aware obligations).
- Explicit eight mutation roots contain 387 current leaf descriptors with actual payloadSchema coordinates; selected exact input closure includes 776 documents/descriptors, of which 389 indexed schema sources have Draft07 declarations. This is 387 leaf payloads plus two transitive glTF snapshot/diff documents. No selected schema uses 2020 here.
- No missing old external-ID obligation found among this selected closure; **78 duplicate `$id` groups exist in the full broad search roots**, none touches selected payload/referenced sources. Old `or_insert` makes those unrelated collisions traversal-order first wins. Feeding the entire broad search tree into strict closure would correctly refuse them even if unused. Do not silently suppress them, or choose one by incidental directory ordering.

## Actual Selected Vocabulary And Reference Spellings

Selected vendor annotation keys are exactly `x-semio-state`, `x-semio-ui`, and `x-semio-formats`. They must be supplied as explicit opaque annotation authority for these selected sources. Do not blanket permit every `x-*`; verify extension semantics before classifying new keys as annotation. `x-semio-inverse-rows` is a separate producer-facing row obligation in current code, but no instance appeared in these selected declared-root inputs; when encountered elsewhere, capture its raw payload separately and explicitly state it is opaque to schema-reference traversal.

Whole broad root vendor vocabulary also contains `x-semio-child-kind`, `x-semio-child-standard`, `x-semio-child-subset`, `x-semio-derived`, `x-semio-invariant`, `x-semio`, `x-semio-child`, `x-semio-link`, `x-semio-owner`. These occur in other indexed schemas/data and cannot be silently added to selected source authority without semantic review.

Selected `$id` spellings use absolute `https://json.schemas.assets.semio-tech.com/...` and must remain the original exact raw field strings. Resource index can canonicalize URI lookup privately but must keep original source untouched. Current glTF cross-document raw references include:

## Leaf4 And MutationsAggregate1 Source Slots

Leaf4 means the four actual quote include slots in current `expand_mutation_leaf`, not four consumers: taxonomy_dependency, descriptor_dependency, payload_schema_dependency, referenced_documents. Current closure integration governs the fourth slot's 0..N ordered physical inputs plus payload source dependency identity. The first three include edges remain independently exact and mandatory. Original producer emits PAYLOAD_SCHEMA_DOCUMENTS includes and later aggregate forwards leaf lists into INPUT_SCHEMA_DOCUMENTS.

MutationsAggregate1 current `expand_derive_mutations` emits quote-repeated dependencies from singleton `[authority.taxonomy_path.clone()]`; its source-derived cardinality is one. It does not itself discover schema documents; the per-leaf dependency lists are forwarded in aggregate output. Keep aggregate taxonomy source include receipt separately and do not fabricate a second schema closure for its singleton taxonomy slot. Source/quote-span/export identity must be exact for both producer variants in generic registry.

## Explicit Neutral Call Authority

For each actual registered leaf consumer:

1. Capture its exact source, descriptor, taxonomy projection and payload schema physical inputs through producer registry's no-follow owned source capture. Validate descriptor's payloadSchema under exact owner before invoking neutral closure. Preserve root raw UTF-8 source, path and source hash.
2. Construct root `{id: exact physical registry identity, path: exact workspace-relative coordinate, baseUri: explicit retrieval URI, source: exact raw text}`. For selected corpus, root `$id` is already absolute and supplies a deliberate retrieval-base choice; explicitly record this choice instead of deriving it from opaque path inside resolver.
3. Provide an explicit finite sibling set from registered physical resource authority, preserving all `$id`/nested IDs/raw source hashes. Selected glTF payloads need exact snapshot and diff documents; no runtime directory fallback or root-only cache. Sources without absolute `$id` elsewhere require explicit retrieval URI authority and relative `$id` policy; opaque `semio.stdio.*` IDs found in broad schema tree must not be confused with absolute resource URI.
4. Call schemaReferenceClosure with dialect draft-07 and exact annotation set `["x-semio-state","x-semio-ui","x-semio-formats"]` for selected inputs, explicit current source/document/coordinate/depth/schema/resource/reference/chunk limits, caller cancellation/progress/yield. Raw source is not reconstructed with JSON.stringify.
5. Save every origin edge/raw reference including repeated/local references; use unique deterministic returned physical documents excluding root to populate slot4 in source-proved order. Record include ordinal/path/source hash and exact root→document relation. Do not dedupe different consumers' include edges or merge source slots.
6. Refuse malformed inputs, duplicate members/IDs, unresolved refs, unsupported dialect/extension/dynamic semantics, budget/cancellation. Unknown old registry lookup must not become a silent absent include. All-object scanning must be removed, not maintained as compatibility fallback; const/default/examples/enum are instance data.

The full broad index has collisions and mixed unrelated vocabularies; establish explicit generic registered schema-resource authority instead of choosing an implicit directory search set. If that authority includes colliding app/config schemas, handcraft unique IDs/assets and fix references all at once. Strictly specifying a selected physical sibling set is legitimate when the generic registry explicitly owns it; filtering conflicts merely because current query does not reach them is not a substitute for authority.

## Small Actual Owned Calls

Two exact current payload samples were invoked against current TS closure with explicit original raw sources and the selected vendor authority. One architect replacement payload accepted with no additional documents; one glTF required-add payload accepted with exact snapshot+diff sources and 167 retained schema-reference edges. Exit 0. This verifies those two concrete calls, not all 387 leaves or native include/dep-info parity.
